CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE QUALITY_CHECK_ERR_VAL
IS
    -- 변수 선언
    RULE_ID_VAL VARCHAR(30);
    TB_NM_VAL VARCHAR(50);
    COL_NM_VAL VARCHAR(50);
    TOT_CNT_VAL VARCHAR(50);
    ERR_CNT_VAL VARCHAR(50);
    ERR_VALUE_VAL VARCHAR(4000);
    CHK_TB1 INTEGER;
    CHK_TB2 INTEGER;
    CHK_SEQ INTEGER;
BEGIN
    BEGIN
        UPDATE RESULT_QUALITY_CHECK SET ERR_VALUE = NULL;
        COMMIT;
    END;
    -- 프로시저 본문
    BEGIN
        WITH query_result AS (
            -- 쿼리 결과를 변수에 저장
            SELECT ERR_VALUE, RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM FROM (
 SELECT DISTINCT SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(PERSON_ID),1,30) AS ERR_VALUE, 'ID1002' AS RULE_ID,  
   'PERSON' AS TB_NM,    
   'PERSON_ID' AS COL_NM 
    FROM YourSchemaName.PERSON A
    WHERE  PERSON_ID IS NULL
AND ROWNUM < 4
) XX

        )
        SELECT
            RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM, ERR_VALUE
        INTO
            RULE_ID_VAL, TB_NM_VAL, COL_NM_VAL, ERR_VALUE_VAL
        FROM query_result;
        -- 결과를 테이블에 삽입
        UPDATE RESULT_QUALITY_CHECK SET ERR_VALUE = ERR_VALUE_VAL WHERE RULE_ID = RULE_ID_VAL;
    EXCEPTION
 WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN NULL;         WHEN OTHERS THEN
            INSERT INTO QUALITY_CHECK_ERROR_LOG (SEQ, CHK_ID, ERROR_MESSAGE)
            VALUES (QUALITY_CHECK_SEQ.NEXTVAL, RULE_ID_VAL, 'An error has occurred.');
    END;
        COMMIT;

    -- 프로시저 본문
    BEGIN
        WITH query_result AS (
            -- 쿼리 결과를 변수에 저장
            SELECT ERR_VALUE, RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM FROM (
 SELECT DISTINCT SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(PERSON_ID),1,30) AS ERR_VALUE, 'ID1003' AS RULE_ID,  
   'PERSON' AS TB_NM,    
   'PERSON_ID' AS COL_NM 
FROM YourSchemaName.PERSON A
WHERE CASE WHEN NOT EXISTS 
(SELECT 1 FROM YourSchemaName.VISIT_OCCURRENCE B WHERE B.PERSON_ID = A.PERSON_ID)  
THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END = 'Y' 
AND ( PERSON_ID IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(TO_CHAR(PERSON_ID))>0 ) 
AND ROWNUM < 4
) XX

        )
        SELECT
            RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM, ERR_VALUE
        INTO
            RULE_ID_VAL, TB_NM_VAL, COL_NM_VAL, ERR_VALUE_VAL
        FROM query_result;
        -- 결과를 테이블에 삽입
        UPDATE RESULT_QUALITY_CHECK SET ERR_VALUE = ERR_VALUE_VAL WHERE RULE_ID = RULE_ID_VAL;
    EXCEPTION
 WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN NULL;         WHEN OTHERS THEN
            INSERT INTO QUALITY_CHECK_ERROR_LOG (SEQ, CHK_ID, ERROR_MESSAGE)
            VALUES (QUALITY_CHECK_SEQ.NEXTVAL, RULE_ID_VAL, 'An error has occurred.');
    END;
        COMMIT;

    -- 프로시저 본문
    BEGIN
        WITH query_result AS (
            -- 쿼리 결과를 변수에 저장
            SELECT ERR_VALUE, RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM FROM (
 SELECT DISTINCT SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(PERSON_ID),1,30) AS ERR_VALUE, 'ID1004' AS RULE_ID,  
   'PERSON' AS TB_NM,    
   'PERSON_ID' AS COL_NM 
FROM YourSchemaName.PERSON A
WHERE CASE WHEN NOT EXISTS 
(SELECT 1 FROM YourSchemaName.PROCEDURE_OCCURRENCE B WHERE B.PERSON_ID = A.PERSON_ID)  
THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END = 'Y' 
AND ( PERSON_ID IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(TO_CHAR(PERSON_ID))>0 ) 
AND ROWNUM < 4
) XX

        )
        SELECT
            RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM, ERR_VALUE
        INTO
            RULE_ID_VAL, TB_NM_VAL, COL_NM_VAL, ERR_VALUE_VAL
        FROM query_result;
        -- 결과를 테이블에 삽입
        UPDATE RESULT_QUALITY_CHECK SET ERR_VALUE = ERR_VALUE_VAL WHERE RULE_ID = RULE_ID_VAL;
    EXCEPTION
 WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN NULL;         WHEN OTHERS THEN
            INSERT INTO QUALITY_CHECK_ERROR_LOG (SEQ, CHK_ID, ERROR_MESSAGE)
            VALUES (QUALITY_CHECK_SEQ.NEXTVAL, RULE_ID_VAL, 'An error has occurred.');
    END;
        COMMIT;

    -- 프로시저 본문
    BEGIN
        WITH query_result AS (
            -- 쿼리 결과를 변수에 저장
            SELECT ERR_VALUE, RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM FROM (
 SELECT DISTINCT SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(PERSON_ID),1,30) AS ERR_VALUE, 'ID1005' AS RULE_ID,  
   'PERSON' AS TB_NM,    
   'PERSON_ID' AS COL_NM 
FROM YourSchemaName.PERSON A
WHERE CASE WHEN NOT EXISTS 
(SELECT 1 FROM YourSchemaName.DEATH B WHERE B.PERSON_ID = A.PERSON_ID
AND TO_DATE(A.YEAR_OF_BIRTH || '-' || 
           LPAD(A.MONTH_OF_BIRTH, 2, '0') || '-' || 
           LPAD(A.DAY_OF_BIRTH, 2, '0'), 'YYYY-MM-DD') < B.DEATH_DATE
)  
THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END = 'Y' 
AND ( PERSON_ID IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(TO_CHAR(PERSON_ID))>0 ) 
AND ROWNUM < 4
) XX

        )
        SELECT
            RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM, ERR_VALUE
        INTO
            RULE_ID_VAL, TB_NM_VAL, COL_NM_VAL, ERR_VALUE_VAL
        FROM query_result;
        -- 결과를 테이블에 삽입
        UPDATE RESULT_QUALITY_CHECK SET ERR_VALUE = ERR_VALUE_VAL WHERE RULE_ID = RULE_ID_VAL;
    EXCEPTION
 WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN NULL;         WHEN OTHERS THEN
            INSERT INTO QUALITY_CHECK_ERROR_LOG (SEQ, CHK_ID, ERROR_MESSAGE)
            VALUES (QUALITY_CHECK_SEQ.NEXTVAL, RULE_ID_VAL, 'An error has occurred.');
    END;
        COMMIT;

    -- 프로시저 본문
    BEGIN
        WITH query_result AS (
            -- 쿼리 결과를 변수에 저장
            SELECT ERR_VALUE, RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM FROM (
 SELECT DISTINCT SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(GENDER_CONCEPT_ID),1,30) AS ERR_VALUE, 'ID1006' AS RULE_ID,  
   'PERSON' AS TB_NM,    
   'GENDER_CONCEPT_ID' AS COL_NM 
FROM YourSchemaName.PERSON A
WHERE CASE WHEN NOT EXISTS 
(SELECT 1 FROM YourSchemaName.CONCEPT B WHERE 1=1 and lower(domain_id) = 'gender' and lower(standard_concept) in ('standard', 's'))  
THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END = 'Y' 
AND ( GENDER_CONCEPT_ID IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(TO_CHAR(GENDER_CONCEPT_ID))>0 )

) XX

        COMMIT;

    END;
        COMMIT;

    -- 프로시저 본문
    BEGIN
        WITH query_result AS (
            -- 쿼리 결과를 변수에 저장
            SELECT ERR_VALUE, RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM FROM (
SELECT DISTINCT SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(YEAR_OF_BIRTH),1,30) AS ERR_VALUE, 'ID1008' AS RULE_ID,  
   'PERSON' AS TB_NM,    
   'YEAR_OF_BIRTH' AS COL_NM 
FROM YourSchemaName.PERSON
WHERE CASE WHEN REGEXP_LIKE(YEAR_OF_BIRTH, '^[0-9]{4}$')
 THEN 'Y' 
 ELSE 'N' 
 END = 'Y' 
AND (YEAR_OF_BIRTH IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(TO_CHAR(YEAR_OF_BIRTH))>0 )
AND ROWNUM < 4
) XX

        )

            RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM, ERR_VALUE

 WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN NULL;         WHEN OTHERS THEN
            INSERT INTO QUALITY_CHECK_ERROR_LOG (SEQ, CHK_ID, ERROR_MESSAGE)

        COMMIT;

            SELECT ERR_VALUE, RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM FROM (
 SELECT DISTINCT SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(YEAR_OF_BIRTH),1,30) AS ERR_VALUE, 'ID1009' AS RULE_ID,

   'PERSON' AS TB_NM,    
   'YEAR_OF_BIRTH' AS COL_NM 
FROM YourSchemaName.PERSON A
WHERE  YEAR_OF_BIRTH IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(TO_CHAR(YEAR_OF_BIRTH)) > 0 
AND YEAR_OF_BIRTH NOT BETWEEN 1900 AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM SYSDATE)
AND ROWNUM < 4
) XX

        )
        SELECT
            RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM, ERR_VALUE
        INTO
            RULE_ID_VAL, TB_NM_VAL, COL_NM_VAL, ERR_VALUE_VAL

AND MONTH_OF_BIRTH NOT BETWEEN 1 AND 12
AND ROWNUM < 4
) XX

        )

            RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM, ERR_VALUE

            INSERT INTO QUALITY_CHECK_ERROR_LOG (SEQ, CHK_ID, ERROR_MESSAGE)

 SELECT DISTINCT SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(ETHNICITY_CONCEPT_ID),1,30) AS ERR_VALUE, 'ID1016' AS RULE_ID,

            -- 쿼리 결과를 변수에 저장
            SELECT ERR_VALUE, RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM FROM (
 SELECT DISTINCT SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(VISIT_DETAIL_CONCEPT_ID),1,30) AS ERR_VALUE, 'ID1071' AS RULE_ID,  
   'VISIT_DETAIL' AS TB_NM,    
   'VISIT_DETAIL_CONCEPT_ID' AS COL_NM 
FROM YourSchemaName.VISIT_DETAIL A
WHERE CASE WHEN NOT EXISTS 
(SELECT 1 FROM YourSchemaName.CONCEPT B WHERE 1=1 and lower(domain_id) = 'visit' and lower(standard_concept) in ('standard', 's'))  
THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END = 'Y' 
AND ( VISIT_DETAIL_CONCEPT_ID IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(TO_CHAR(VISIT_DETAIL_CONCEPT_ID))>0 ) 
AND ROWNUM < 4
) XX

        )
        SELECT
            RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM, ERR_VALUE
        INTO
            RULE_ID_VAL, TB_NM_VAL, COL_NM_VAL, ERR_VALUE_VAL
        FROM query_result;
        -- 결과를 테이블에 삽입
        UPDATE RESULT_QUALITY_CHECK SET ERR_VALUE = ERR_VALUE_VAL WHERE RULE_ID = RULE_ID_VAL;
    EXCEPTION
 WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN NULL;         WHEN OTHERS THEN
            INSERT INTO QUALITY_CHECK_ERROR_LOG (SEQ, CHK_ID, ERROR_MESSAGE)
            VALUES (QUALITY_CHECK_SEQ.NEXTVAL, RULE_ID_VAL, 'An error has occurred.');
    END;

   'CONDITION_OCCURRENCE' AS TB_NM,    
   'CONDITION_STATUS_CONCEPT_ID' AS COL_NM 
FROM YourSchemaName.CONDITION_OCCURRENCE A
WHERE CASE WHEN NOT EXISTS 
(SELECT 1 FROM YourSchemaName.CONCEPT B WHERE 1=1 and lower(domain_id) = 'condition status' and lower(standard_concept) in ('standard', 's'))  
THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END = 'Y' 
AND ( CONDITION_STATUS_CONCEPT_ID IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(TO_CHAR(CONDITION_STATUS_CONCEPT_ID))>0 ) 
AND ROWNUM < 4
) XX

        )
        SELECT
            RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM, ERR_VALUE
        INTO
            RULE_ID_VAL, TB_NM_VAL, COL_NM_VAL, ERR_VALUE_VAL
        FROM query_result;
        -- 결과를 테이블에 삽입
        UPDATE RESULT_QUALITY_CHECK SET ERR_VALUE = ERR_VALUE_VAL WHERE RULE_ID = RULE_ID_VAL;
    EXCEPTION
 WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN NULL;         WHEN OTHERS THEN
            INSERT INTO QUALITY_CHECK_ERROR_LOG (SEQ, CHK_ID, ERROR_MESSAGE)
            VALUES (QUALITY_CHECK_SEQ.NEXTVAL, RULE_ID_VAL, 'An error has occurred.');

            -- 쿼리 결과를 변수에 저장
            SELECT ERR_VALUE, RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM FROM (
 SELECT DISTINCT SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(MEASUREMENT_TYPE_CONCEPT_ID),1,30) AS ERR_VALUE, 'ID1216' AS RULE_ID,  
   'MEASUREMENT' AS TB_NM,    
   'MEASUREMENT_TYPE_CONCEPT_ID' AS COL_NM 
    FROM YourSchemaName.MEASUREMENT A
    WHERE  MEASUREMENT_TYPE_CONCEPT_ID IS NULL
AND ROWNUM < 4
) XX

        )
        SELECT
            RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM, ERR_VALUE
        INTO
            RULE_ID_VAL, TB_NM_VAL, COL_NM_VAL, ERR_VALUE_VAL
        FROM query_result;
        -- 결과를 테이블에 삽입
        UPDATE RESULT_QUALITY_CHECK SET ERR_VALUE = ERR_VALUE_VAL WHERE RULE_ID = RULE_ID_VAL;
    EXCEPTION
 WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN NULL;         WHEN OTHERS THEN
            INSERT INTO QUALITY_CHECK_ERROR_LOG (SEQ, CHK_ID, ERROR_MESSAGE)
            VALUES (QUALITY_CHECK_SEQ.NEXTVAL, RULE_ID_VAL, 'An error has occurred.');
    END;
        COMMIT;

 SELECT DISTINCT SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(OBSERVATION_DATE),1,30) AS ERR_VALUE, 'ID1241' AS RULE_ID,  
   'OBSERVATION' AS TB_NM,    
   'OBSERVATION_DATE' AS COL_NM 
FROM YourSchemaName.OBSERVATION A
WHERE CASE WHEN NOT EXISTS 
(SELECT 1 FROM YourSchemaName.VISIT_OCCURRENCE B WHERE B.VISIT_OCCURRENCE_ID = A.VISIT_OCCURRENCE_ID
AND A.OBSERVATION_DATE BETWEEN (B.VISIT_START_DATE - 7) 
                                  AND (B.VISIT_START_DATE + 7)
)  
THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END = 'Y' 
AND ( OBSERVATION_DATE IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(TO_CHAR(OBSERVATION_DATE))>0 ) 
AND ROWNUM < 4
) XX

        )
        SELECT
            RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM, ERR_VALUE
        INTO
            RULE_ID_VAL, TB_NM_VAL, COL_NM_VAL, ERR_VALUE_VAL
        FROM query_result;
        -- 결과를 테이블에 삽입
        UPDATE RESULT_QUALITY_CHECK SET ERR_VALUE = ERR_VALUE_VAL WHERE RULE_ID = RULE_ID_VAL;
    EXCEPTION
 WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN NULL;         WHEN OTHERS THEN
            INSERT INTO QUALITY_CHECK_ERROR_LOG (SEQ, CHK_ID, ERROR_MESSAGE)
            VALUES (QUALITY_CHECK_SEQ.NEXTVAL, RULE_ID_VAL, 'An error has occurred.');
    END;
        COMMIT;

   'DEATH' AS TB_NM,    
   'PERSON_ID' AS COL_NM 
FROM (
   SELECT
      PERSON_ID,
      COUNT(*) OVER (PARTITION BY PERSON_ID) AS CNT
    FROM YourSchemaName.DEATH A
    WHERE  PERSON_ID IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(TO_CHAR(PERSON_ID)) > 0 
         ) subquery
WHERE CNT > 1
AND ROWNUM < 4
) XX

        )

            RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM, ERR_VALUE

(SELECT 1 FROM YourSchemaName.CONCEPT B WHERE B.CONCEPT_ID = A.CAUSE_CONCEPT_ID)  
THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END = 'Y' 
AND ( CAUSE_CONCEPT_ID IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(TO_CHAR(CAUSE_CONCEPT_ID))>0 ) 
AND CAUSE_CONCEPT_ID != 0
AND ROWNUM < 4
) XX

        )
        SELECT
            RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM, ERR_VALUE
        INTO
            RULE_ID_VAL, TB_NM_VAL, COL_NM_VAL, ERR_VALUE_VAL
        FROM query_result;
        -- 결과를 테이블에 삽입
        UPDATE RESULT_QUALITY_CHECK SET ERR_VALUE = ERR_VALUE_VAL WHERE RULE_ID = RULE_ID_VAL;
    EXCEPTION
 WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN NULL;         WHEN OTHERS THEN
            INSERT INTO QUALITY_CHECK_ERROR_LOG (SEQ, CHK_ID, ERROR_MESSAGE)
            VALUES (QUALITY_CHECK_SEQ.NEXTVAL, RULE_ID_VAL, 'An error has occurred.');
    END;
        COMMIT;

 SELECT DISTINCT SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(SPECIMEN_DATE),1,30) AS ERR_VALUE, 'ID1319' AS RULE_ID,  
   'SPECIMEN' AS TB_NM,    
   'SPECIMEN_DATE' AS COL_NM 
    FROM YourSchemaName.SPECIMEN A
    WHERE  SPECIMEN_DATE IS NULL
AND ROWNUM < 4
) XX

        )
        SELECT
            RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM, ERR_VALUE
        INTO
            RULE_ID_VAL, TB_NM_VAL, COL_NM_VAL, ERR_VALUE_VAL
        FROM query_result;
        -- 결과를 테이블에 삽입
        UPDATE RESULT_QUALITY_CHECK SET ERR_VALUE = ERR_VALUE_VAL WHERE RULE_ID = RULE_ID_VAL;
    EXCEPTION
 WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN NULL;         WHEN OTHERS THEN
            INSERT INTO QUALITY_CHECK_ERROR_LOG (SEQ, CHK_ID, ERROR_MESSAGE)
            VALUES (QUALITY_CHECK_SEQ.NEXTVAL, RULE_ID_VAL, 'An error has occurred.');
    END;
        COMMIT;

    -- 프로시저 본문
    BEGIN
        WITH query_result AS (
            -- 쿼리 결과를 변수에 저장
            SELECT ERR_VALUE, RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM FROM (
 SELECT DISTINCT SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(SPECIMEN_DATE),1,30) AS ERR_VALUE, 'ID1320' AS RULE_ID,  
   'SPECIMEN' AS TB_NM,    
   'SPECIMEN_DATE' AS COL_NM 
FROM YourSchemaName.SPECIMEN A
WHERE CASE WHEN NOT EXISTS 
(SELECT 1 FROM YourSchemaName.DEATH B WHERE B.PERSON_ID = A.PERSON_ID
AND A.SPECIMEN_DATE < B.DEATH_DATE
)  
THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END = 'Y' 
AND ( SPECIMEN_DATE IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(TO_CHAR(SPECIMEN_DATE))>0 ) 
AND ROWNUM < 4
) XX

        )
        SELECT
            RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM, ERR_VALUE
        INTO
            RULE_ID_VAL, TB_NM_VAL, COL_NM_VAL, ERR_VALUE_VAL
        FROM query_result;
        -- 결과를 테이블에 삽입
        UPDATE RESULT_QUALITY_CHECK SET ERR_VALUE = ERR_VALUE_VAL WHERE RULE_ID = RULE_ID_VAL;
    EXCEPTION
 WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN NULL;         WHEN OTHERS THEN

            VALUES (QUALITY_CHECK_SEQ.NEXTVAL, RULE_ID_VAL, 'An error has occurred.');
    END;
        COMMIT;

            SELECT ERR_VALUE, RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM FROM (
SELECT DISTINCT SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(SPECIMEN_DATETIME),1,30) AS ERR_VALUE, 'ID1321' AS RULE_ID,  
   'SPECIMEN' AS TB_NM,    
   'SPECIMEN_DATETIME' AS COL_NM 
FROM YourSchemaName.SPECIMEN
WHERE CASE WHEN NOT REGEXP_LIKE(SPECIMEN_DATETIME, SPECIMEN_DATE)
 THEN 'Y' 
 ELSE 'N' 
 END = 'Y' 
AND (SPECIMEN_DATETIME IS NOT NULL AND LENGTH(TO_CHAR(SPECIMEN_DATETIME))>0 )
AND ROWNUM < 4
) XX

        )
        SELECT
            RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM, ERR_VALUE

AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(QUANTITY, '^[-+]?[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?$')
AND ROWNUM < 4
) XX

            RULE_ID, TB_NM, COL_NM, ERR_VALUE
